Content note: The opposition's dossier on the awakening: Chauncy's five-part attack on its enthusiasm, itinerancy and disorders, built from his own tour of the colonies and from letters and documents he prints; the census's hostile source, full of revival data.

Some  particular  Irregularities  have  been  vindi 
cated  as  Things  right  and  good.  But  how  ?

plainers  of  thefe  Things,  inftead  of  fixing  it  where 
it  juftly  belonged,  upon  theThings  themfelves.  To 
illiterate  this  in  a  few  Inilances.
